📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

狂犬病病毒本期文献呈现多方向研究进展

本期重点文献按发表日期、相关性、证据等级和研究质量综合排序，研究结果与综述证据分别核验后形成以下进展。

  • 在检查的 51 例疑似病例中,46 例(90.2%)经实验室确诊为狂犬病。家养狗占提交内容的 96.1%,而猫则占 3.9%。大多数可疑动物要么未接种疫苗(64.7%),要么疫苗接种状况未知(33.3%),据报道,90.2%的动物在提交前曾咬伤过人类。 [paper-963b1a18a14523fa]
  • 确定了六个主要主题: 狂犬病死亡焦虑。医疗焦虑和寻求安慰。家庭保护和社会影响。污染恐惧和避免。以及对自由漫步的狗的有争议的态度。死亡焦虑(26%)和寻求医疗保证(30%)是最常见的主题类别。 [paper-78c12539ae601d62]
  • 尸检显示脑组织中存在病毒抗原(通过直接荧光抗体 31 测试)和病毒 RNA(通过实时 RTPCR)。 [paper-784af4221daac0f4]
  • 八项实地随机对照试验 (RCT) 表明,与对照相比,One Health 干预措施有效减少了疾病发生或病原体检测。典型的做法包括全面的多领域干预措施、参与性方法、长期结果评估、方法三角测量、对结果的谨慎解释以及描述干预措施的视觉辅助工具。 [paper-6cc8d7c282a20794]
  • 延误医疗护理与发烧(65.6%)为主要症状有关。 [paper-d4460c2146ff116f]

本期研究方向以入选文献实际覆盖的临床、流行病学、宿主生态、诊断或分子监测主题为准。

📘 研究型文献 / Research Articles

Journal: Tropical Medicine and Health  |  Online: 2026-07-13  |  Published: 2026-07-13  |  Print: 2026-07-13  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-13  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  DOI: 10.1186/s41182-026-01027-0  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E2

对狂犬病的恐惧和被咬后的心理压力:对印度 YouTube 评论的定性研究

Fear of rabies and post-bite psychological stress: a qualitative study of YouTube comments from India
作者： Harish Kumar Tiwari, Parimala Mohanty, Arindam Biswas, Jully Gogoi-Tiwari, H. Tiwari, J. Gogoi-Tiwari
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 狗介导的狂犬病仍然是一种致命但可预防的人畜共患疾病,并且在印度继续造成全球狂犬病死亡的很大一部分。尽管生物医学和流行病学方面已有详细记录,但对狂犬病暴露的心理影响以及公众对其的恐惧却很少关注。目的 探讨印度公共网络话语中对狂犬病的认知和情感体验,并描述被狗咬伤后表达的焦虑和痛苦模式。方法 从 6 个印度 YouTube 视频中检索公众评论 (n = 300),其中包含狂犬病相关内容,包括新闻辩论、医学解释、致命病例叙述、自由漫步的狗攻击镜头以及涉及暴露后预防 (PEP) 的短视频。在筛选相关性后,使用 Braun 和 Clarke 的六阶段归纳主题分析框架对 172 条评论进行了分析。结果 确定了六个主要主题:狂犬病死亡焦虑;医疗焦虑和寻求安慰;家庭保护和社会影响;污染恐惧和避免;高度警惕;以及对自由漫步的狗的有争议的态度。以恐惧为导向的主题占主导地位。死亡焦虑(26%)和寻求医疗保证(30%)是最常见的主题类别。许多评论描述了持续的沉思、反复的伤口检查、疫苗充足性的不确定性以及接触后很长时间内持续的痛苦。还描述了家庭层面的行为变化和回避做法。评论还揭示了对自由漫游狗的不同看法,从支持人道疫苗接种、绝育和共存到呼吁移除或扑杀。结论 经过分析的社交媒体言论表明,狂犬病不仅被视为生物医学威胁,而且也是持续不确定性和恐惧的根源。数字评论空间揭示了不确定性、对死亡的恐惧以及感知暴露后的行为适应。将心理考虑和明确的风险沟通纳入狂犬病控制计划可能会增强公众对预防和治疗策略的信心。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狗介导的狂犬病仍然是一种致命但可预防的人畜共患疾病,在印度导致全球狂犬病死亡的很大一部分,但暴露和公众恐惧的心理影响尚未得到充分研究。该研究旨在探讨印度公众在线讨论中如何看待狂犬病和在情感上体验狂犬病,并描述被狗咬伤后表达的焦虑和痛苦模式。
设计与对象
对从六个包含狂犬病相关内容的印度 YouTube 视频中检索到的公众评论进行了定性主题分析。经过相关性筛选后,总共对 172 条评论进行了分析。
核心方法
公众评论 (n = 300) 是从 6 个印度 YouTube 视频中检索的,包括新闻辩论、医学解释、致命病例叙述、自由漫步的狗攻击镜头以及涉及暴露后预防的短视频。使用布劳恩和克拉克的六阶段归纳框架进行主题分析。
主要结果
确定了六个主要主题: 狂犬病死亡焦虑。医疗焦虑和寻求安慰。家庭保护和社会影响。污染恐惧和避免。以及对自由漫步的狗的有争议的态度。死亡焦虑(26%)和寻求医疗保证(30%)是最常见的主题类别。
解释与创新
该研究将狂犬病解释为超出其生物医学威胁的持续不确定性和恐惧的根源,强调数字评论空间如何揭示感知暴露后持续的痛苦和行为适应。这种对印度狂犬病公众讨论的定性探索是新颖的,它关注的是心理影响而不是临床或流行病学方面。
科研与公卫意义
将心理考虑和明确的风险沟通纳入狂犬病控制计划可能会增强公众对预防和治疗策略的信心。研究结果表明,解决恐惧和不确定性可以提高对暴露后预防的依从性并减少不必要的痛苦。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖 YouTube 评论而受到限制,这些评论可能无法代表更广泛的人群,并且会受到选择和报告偏差的影响。由于分析的定性性质以及缺乏与其他数据源的定量验证或三角测量,证据强度中等。

Fear of rabies and post-bite psychological stress: a qualitative study of YouTube comments from India

Authors: Harish Kumar Tiwari, Parimala Mohanty, Arindam Biswas, Jully Gogoi-Tiwari, H. Tiwari, J. Gogoi-Tiwari
Original Abstract
Abstract Background Dog-mediated rabies remains a fatal yet preventable zoonotic disease and continues to cause a substantial proportion of global rabies deaths in India. Although biomedical and epidemiological aspects are well documented, far less attention has been paid to the psychological impact of rabies exposure and the public fear surrounding it. Objective To explore how rabies is perceived and emotionally experienced in public online discourse in India, and to characterise patterns of anxiety and distress expressed following dog-bite exposure. Methods Public comments (n = 300) were retrieved from six Indian YouTube videos featuring rabies-related content, including news debates, medical explanations, fatal case narratives, free-roaming-dog attack footage, and short videos referencing post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP). Following screening for relevance, 172 comments were analysed using Braun and Clarke’s six-phase inductive thematic analysis framework. Results Six principal themes were identified: Rabies Death Anxiety; Medical Anxiety and Reassurance Seeking; Family Protection and Social Impact; Contamination Fear and Avoidance; Hypervigilance; and Contested Attitudes towards Free-Roaming Dogs. Fear-oriented themes predominated. Death anxiety (26%) and medical reassurance seeking (30%) were the most frequent thematic categories. Many comments described persistent rumination, repeated wound-checking, uncertainty regarding vaccine adequacy, and ongoing distress long after exposure. Family-level behavioural changes and avoidance practices were also described. Comments also revealed contrasting views on free-roaming dogs, ranging from support for humane vaccination, sterilisation and coexistence to calls for removal or culling. Conclusion The analysed social media discourse suggests that rabies is represented not only as a biomedical threat, but also as a source of persistent uncertainty and fear. Digital comment spaces reveal uncertainty, fear of fatality, and behavioural adaptation following perceived exposure. Integrating psychological considerations and clear risk communication into rabies-control programmes may strengthen public confidence in prevention and treatment strategies.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E2

来自一只 54 天大、表现不典型的小狗的类似北极 1a 狂犬病病毒的特征,印度浦那,2026 年

Characterization of an Arctic-like 1a rabies virus from a 54-day-old puppy with atypical presentation, Pune, India, 2026
作者： Padinjaremattathil Thankappan Ullas, Vikas Sharma, Veena Vipat, Shweta Choudhary, Alfia Fathima Ashraf, Ranjana Mariyam Raju, Venu Kotturi, Kunal Sudhakar Sakhare, Vijay Parashramji Bondre, Shweta Choudhari
摘要中文翻译摘要: 在印度,狂犬病仍然是一种严重未报告的致命人畜共患疾病,该国狗群中以类北极 1a (AL1a) 谱系为主。虽然狗的非典型临床表现可能会延误诊断并增加人类接触风险,但新生犬狂犬病的基因组和临床数据仍然有限。这项研究报告了一只 54 天大的未接种疫苗的德国牧羊犬幼犬感染狂犬病的特殊病例,该幼犬出现严重瘙痒和自咬行为。由于临床反应不佳,这只小狗被安乐死。尸检显示脑组织中存在病毒抗原(通过直接荧光抗体测试)和病毒 RNA(通过实时 RTPCR)。全基因组测序恢复了近乎完整的狂犬病病毒基因组(11,947 个核苷酸;99.5% 基因组覆盖率),属于 AL1a_A1.1 亚系。系统发育分析揭示了与当代印度狂犬病病毒株的密切遗传相关性。比较基因组分析在磷蛋白、基质、糖蛋白和聚合酶基因中分别鉴定出 4、3、6 和 8 个非同义取代。该病例是有记录的最年轻的具有非典型表现的犬狂犬病病例之一,由 AL1a 病毒分支引起。我们的研究结果强调了与新生儿犬狂犬病相关的风险、对非典型病例加强临床怀疑的必要性,以及基因组监测对监测狂犬病流行地区不断演变的狂犬病病毒谱系的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
虽然狗的非典型临床表现可能会延迟 27 诊断并增加人类暴露风险,但新生犬狂犬病的基因组和临床数据仍然有限 28。
设计与对象
40 关键词:狂犬病、犬狂犬病、小狗、非典型临床表现、全基因组测序、41 类北极 1a 谱系 42 简介 43 狂犬病是一种严重低估的致命人畜共患脑脊髓炎,由弹状病毒科 44 狂犬病病毒属 RNA 病毒引起。
核心方法
全基因组测序恢复了近乎完整的狂犬病病毒基因组(11,947 个核苷酸。
主要结果
尸检显示脑组织中存在病毒抗原(通过直接荧光抗体 31 测试)和病毒 RNA(通过实时 RTPCR)。
解释与创新
系统发育分析揭示了与当代 34 种印度狂犬病病毒株的密切遗传相关性。
科研与公卫意义
我们的研究结果强调了与新生儿犬狂犬病相关的风险、对非典型病例加强临床怀疑的必要性,以及基因组监测对监测狂犬病流行地区不断演变的狂犬病病毒谱系的重要性。
局限与证据强度
18 这些作者也对这项工作做出了同样的贡献。

Characterization of an Arctic-like 1a rabies virus from a 54-day-old puppy with atypical presentation, Pune, India, 2026

Authors: Padinjaremattathil Thankappan Ullas, Vikas Sharma, Veena Vipat, Shweta Choudhary, Alfia Fathima Ashraf, Ranjana Mariyam Raju, Venu Kotturi, Kunal Sudhakar Sakhare, Vijay Parashramji Bondre, Shweta Choudhari
Original Abstract
Abstract Rabies remains a significantly underreported fatal zoonosis in India, where the Arctic-like 1a (AL1a) lineage predominates in dog populations. While atypical clinical presentations in dogs can delay diagnosis and increase human exposure risk, genomic and clinical data on neonatal canine rabies remain limited. This study reports an exceptional case of rabies in a 54-day old unvaccinated German shepherd puppy which presented with severe pruritus and self-biting behaviour. The puppy was euthanized due to poor clinical response. Post-mortem testing revealed viral antigen (by Direct Fluorescent Antibody Test) and viral RNA (by real-time RTPCR) in the brain tissue. Whole-genome sequencing recovered a near-complete rabies virus genome (11,947 nucleotides; 99.5% genome coverage), classified within the AL1a_A1.1 sublineage. Phylogenetic analysis revealed close genetic relatedness to contemporary Indian rabies virus strains. Comparative genomic analysis identified 4, 3, 6, and 8 non-synonymous substitutions in the phosphoprotein, matrix, glycoprotein, and polymerase genes, respectively. This case is one of the youngest documented cases of canine rabies with atypical manifestations, caused by the AL1a viral clade. Our findings highlight the risks associated with neonatal canine rabies, the need for heightened clinical suspicion in atypical cases, and the importance of genomic surveillance to monitor evolving rabies virus lineages in endemic regions.

加纳博诺东部地区动物狂犬病实验室监测:六年回顾性研究(2020 – 2025 年)

Laboratory-Based Surveillance of Animal Rabies in the Bono East Region of Ghana: A Six-Year Retrospective Study (2020 – 2025)
作者： Prince Kyere Dwaah, Sylvia Afriyie Squire, Helen Djang-Fordjour, Issah Bagulo, Anthony Osei-Tutu, Samuel Opokuware, Emmanuel Opoku, Roger Twum Gyarko, David Kando, Abraham Num
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 狗介导的狂犬病仍然是一种被忽视的主要热带疾病,也是撒哈拉以南非洲地区可预防的人类死亡的一个重要原因。尽管加纳正在进行控制工作,但实验室确认的监测数据有限,阻碍了对疾病负担的准确评估和基于证据的干预措施的发展。本研究调查了疑似动物提交的实验室确诊狂犬病病例的流行病学特征,并评估了常规诊断监测对狂犬病控制的贡献。方法/主要结果 使用 2020 年至 2025 年间提交给加纳地区兽医实验室的疑似狂犬病病例的实验室监测记录进行回顾性分析。使用塞勒染色、直接荧光抗体测试 (FAT) 和聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 对脑组织样本进行检查。提取流行病学信息并进行描述性分析,包括动物种类、疫苗接种史、所有权状况、咬伤史和诊断结果。在检查的 51 例疑似病例中,46 例(90.2%)经实验室确诊为狂犬病。家养狗占提交内容的 96.1%,而猫则占 3.9%。大多数可疑动物要么未接种疫苗(64.7%),要么疫苗接种状况未知(33.3%),据报道,90.2%的动物在提交前曾咬伤过人类。在整个研究期间发现了确诊病例,表明研究区域内持续存在病毒传播和持续的人畜共患风险。结论/意义 实验室确诊的狂犬病病例比例持续较高,表明狂犬病病毒在家畜中持续传播,并凸显了对人类健康的重大风险。传统和先进实验室诊断方法的结合加强了监测并改善了病例确认。这些发现强调迫切需要扩大狗的疫苗接种覆盖范围、加强基于实验室的监测、改进咬伤报告系统以及加强兽医和公共卫生部门之间的合作。加强综合“同一个健康”战略对于加快实现到 2030 年消除由狗传播的人类狂犬病这一全球目标至关重要。作者摘要 狂犬病仍然是世界上最古老、最具破坏性的人畜共患疾病之一,这种疾病致命但可以预防,每年导致数千人死亡,特别是在非洲和亚洲。在加纳,家犬是狂犬病病毒的主要宿主,但许多地区的常规实验室监测提供的动物狂犬病流行病学信息有限。我们回顾了波诺东地区泰奇曼地区兽医实验室六年(2020 年至 2025 年)的实验室记录,以描述疑似动物狂犬病病例和实验室确诊感染的流行病学特征。在提交诊断的 51 份脑样本中,46 份(90.2%)被证实狂犬病呈阳性。家犬几乎占所有提交的病例,大多数确诊病例涉及未接种疫苗或疫苗接种史未知的动物。此外,据报道,大多数可疑动物在实验室调查之前就曾咬伤过人类,这凸显了犬类狂犬病对公共健康造成的持续风险。该研究还记录了如何通过与国家参考实验室合作加强区域诊断能力,从而能够常规使用国际推荐的诊断方法,例如荧光抗体测试和聚合酶链反应。这些发现为支持加强狂犬病监测、增加大规模狗疫苗接种、提高公众意识和加强“同一个健康”合作提供了宝贵的证据。这些措施对于减少人类狂犬病死亡和支持加纳为到 2030 年消除由狗传播的人类狂犬病全球目标做出贡献至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狗介导的狂犬病是一种被忽视的主要热带疾病,导致撒哈拉以南非洲地区可预防的人类死亡,有限的实验室确认监测数据阻碍了加纳疾病负担的准确评估和循证干预措施的制定。本研究调查了疑似动物提交的实验室确诊狂犬病病例的流行病学特征,并评估了常规诊断监测对狂犬病控制的贡献。
设计与对象
使用 2020 年至 2025 年间提交给加纳地区兽医实验室的疑似狂犬病病例的实验室监测记录进行回顾性分析。该研究检查了 51 例疑似病例,主要来自家养的狗和猫。
核心方法
使用塞勒氏染色、直接荧光抗体测试(FAT)和聚合酶链反应(PCR)检查脑组织样本。提取流行病学信息并进行描述性分析,包括动物种类、疫苗接种史、所有权状况、咬伤史和诊断结果。
主要结果
在检查的 51 例疑似病例中,46 例(90.2%)经实验室确诊为狂犬病。家养狗占提交内容的 96.1%,而猫则占 3.9%。大多数可疑动物要么未接种疫苗(64.7%),要么疫苗接种状况未知(33.3%),据报道,90.2%的动物在提交前曾咬伤过人类。
解释与创新
实验室确诊的狂犬病病例比例持续较高,表明狂犬病病毒在家畜中持续传播,并凸显了对人类健康的巨大风险。传统和先进实验室诊断方法的结合加强了监测并改善了病例确认。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,迫切需要扩大狗的疫苗接种覆盖范围,加强基于实验室的监测,改进咬伤报告系统,并加强兽医和公共卫生部门之间的合作。加强综合“同一个健康”战略对于加速实现到 2030 年消除由狗传播的人类狂犬病这一全球目标至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性和对实验室监测记录的依赖而受到限制,而实验室监测记录可能无法涵盖所有​​病例。然而,高确诊率和详细的流行病学数据提供了狂犬病持续传播的有力证据以及加强控制措施的必要性。

Laboratory-Based Surveillance of Animal Rabies in the Bono East Region of Ghana: A Six-Year Retrospective Study (2020 – 2025)

Authors: Prince Kyere Dwaah, Sylvia Afriyie Squire, Helen Djang-Fordjour, Issah Bagulo, Anthony Osei-Tutu, Samuel Opokuware, Emmanuel Opoku, Roger Twum Gyarko, David Kando, Abraham Num
Original Abstract
Abstract Background Dog-mediated rabies remains a major neglected tropical disease and an important cause of preventable human mortality in sub-Saharan Africa. Despite ongoing control efforts, limited laboratory-confirmed surveillance data hinder accurate assessment of the disease burden and the development of evidence-based interventions in Ghana. This study investigated the epidemiological characteristics of laboratory-confirmed rabies cases among suspected animal submissions and evaluated the contribution of routine diagnostic surveillance to rabies control. Methodology/Principal Findings A retrospective analysis was conducted using laboratory surveillance records of suspected rabies cases submitted to a regional veterinary laboratory in Ghana between 2020 and 2025. Brain tissue samples were examined using Seller’s staining, the direct fluorescent antibody test (FAT), and polymerase chain reaction (PCR). Epidemiological information, including animal species, vaccination history, ownership status, bite history, and diagnostic outcomes, was extracted and analysed descriptively. Of the 51 suspected cases examined, 46 (90.2%) were laboratory confirmed as rabies. Domestic dogs accounted for 96.1% of submissions, while cats represented 3.9%. Most suspected animals were either unvaccinated (64.7%) or had unknown vaccination status (33.3%), and 90.2% had reportedly bitten humans before submission. Confirmed cases were detected throughout the study period, indicating sustained viral circulation and continued zoonotic risk within the study area. Conclusions/Significance The consistently high proportion of laboratory-confirmed rabies cases demonstrates persistent transmission of rabies virus among domestic animals and highlights substantial risks to human health. The integration of conventional and advanced laboratory diagnostic methods strengthened surveillance and improved case confirmation. These findings underscore the urgent need to expand dog vaccination coverage, strengthen laboratory-based surveillance, improve bite reporting systems, and enhance collaboration between veterinary and public health sectors. Reinforcing integrated One Health strategies will be essential to accelerate progress towards the global goal of eliminating dog-mediated human rabies by 2030. Author Summary Rabies remains one of the world’s oldest and most devastating zoonotic diseases, which is fatal yet preventable, causing thousands of human deaths each year, particularly in Africa and Asia. In Ghana, domestic dogs are the principal reservoir of the rabies virus, but information on the epidemiology of animal rabies from routine laboratory surveillance is limited in many regions. We reviewed six years (2020 – 2025) of laboratory records from the Regional Veterinary Laboratory in Techiman, Bono East Region, to describe the epidemiological characteristics of suspected animal rabies cases and laboratory-confirmed infections. Of the 51 brain samples submitted for diagnosis, 46 (90.2%) were confirmed positive for rabies. Domestic dogs accounted for almost all submissions, and most confirmed cases involved animals that were either unvaccinated or had an unknown vaccination history. Furthermore, most suspected animals had reportedly bitten humans before laboratory investigation, highlighting the continuing public health risk posed by canine rabies. The study also documents how regional diagnostic capacity was strengthened through collaboration with national reference laboratories, enabling the routine use of internationally recommended diagnostic methods such as the fluorescent antibody test and polymerase chain reaction. These findings provide valuable evidence to support enhanced rabies surveillance, increased mass dog vaccination, improved public awareness, and strengthened One Health collaboration. Such measures are essential for reducing human rabies deaths and supporting Ghana’s contribution to the global goal of eliminating dog-mediated human rabies by 2030.

CAMKV 是一种 ISG,通过 SQSTM1 介导的选择性自噬促进狂犬病病毒磷蛋白的降解

CAMKV is an ISG and facilitates the degradation of rabies virus phosphoprotein via SQSTM1-mediated selective autophagy
作者： Jinming Wang, Hong Huo, Yuting Tao, Jinliang Wang, Xijun Wang, Zhiyuan Wen, Jinying Ge, Weiye Chen, Zhigao Bu, Lei Shuai
摘要中文翻译一旦狂犬病病毒(RABV)进入中枢神经系统,感染几乎不可避免地会导致致命的结果,而我们对病毒发病机制的不完全了解仍然是有效治疗干预的主要障碍。在这里,我们将 CAMKV 确定为干扰素刺激基因 (ISG),可驱动 RABV 磷蛋白 (P) 的巨自噬/自噬降解,从而有效抑制体外病毒复制。值得注意的是,CAMKV 的体内过度表达显着延缓了接受 RABV 街头毒株攻击的小鼠的疾病进展。从机制上讲,CAMKV 与 RABV P 和 SQSTM1 相互作用,促进 SQSTM1 介导的 P 选择性自噬清除,从而限制 RABV 转录和复制。总的来说,我们的研究结果表明 CAMKV 是一种关键的宿主抗病毒效应子,通过选择性自噬发挥作用,强调 CAMKV 作为开发针对致命性 RABV 感染的新型疗法的有前景的分子靶标。缩写:3-MA:3-甲基腺嘌呤;3-MA:3-甲基腺嘌呤;ABLV:澳大利亚蝙蝠狂犬病毒;ATG:自噬相关;AKT:AKT 丝氨酸/苏氨酸激酶;Baf-A1:巴弗洛霉素A1;CAMKV:CaM 激酶样囊泡相关;CAMK2:钙/钙调蛋白依赖性蛋白激酶 II;co-IP:免疫共沉淀;CQ:氯喹;DUVV:杜文哈基病毒;DMSO:二甲亚砜;EBLV-1:欧洲蝙蝠狂犬病毒1型;ISG:干扰素刺激基因;LAMP1:溶酶体相关膜蛋白1;MAP1LC3/LC3:微管相关蛋白1轻链3;Mdivi-1:线粒体分裂抑制剂-1;MLD50:50%小鼠致死剂量;MOI:感染复数;MTOR:雷帕霉素激酶的机制靶点;qPCR:实时定量聚合酶链式反应;RABV:狂犬病病毒;SQSTM1/p62: 隔离体 1;WT:野生型。

CAMKV is an ISG and facilitates the degradation of rabies virus phosphoprotein via SQSTM1-mediated selective autophagy

Authors: Jinming Wang, Hong Huo, Yuting Tao, Jinliang Wang, Xijun Wang, Zhiyuan Wen, Jinying Ge, Weiye Chen, Zhigao Bu, Lei Shuai
Original Abstract
Once rabies virus (RABV) gains access to the central nervous system, infection almost inevitably results in fatal outcomes, and our incomplete understanding of viral pathogenesis remains a major barrier to effective therapeutic intervention. Here, we identify CAMKV as an interferon-stimulated gene (ISG) that drives the macroautophagic/autophagic degradation of RABV phosphoprotein (P), thereby potently suppressing viral replication in vitro. Notably, in vivo overexpression of CAMKV significantly delays disease progression in mice challenged with a street strain of RABV. Mechanistically, CAMKV interacts with both RABV P and SQSTM1, promoting SQSTM1-mediated selective autophagic clearance of P and thereby restricting RABV transcription and replication. Collectively, our findings establish CAMKV as a critical host antiviral effector that functions through selective autophagy, highlighting CAMKV as a promising molecular target for the development of novel therapeutics against lethal RABV infection.Abbreviations: 3-MA: 3-methyladenine; ABLV: Australian bat lyssavirus; ATG: autophagy related; AKT: AKT serine/threonine kinase; Baf-A1: bafilomycin A1; CAMKV: CaM kinase like vesicle associated; CAMK2: calcium/calmodulin dependent protein kinase II; co-IP: co-immunoprecipitation; CQ: chloroquine; DUVV: Duvenhage virus; DMSO: dimethyl sulfoxide; EBLV-1: European bat lyssavirus 1; ISG: interferon stimulated gene; LAMP1: lysosome associated membrane protein 1; MAP1LC3/LC3: microtubule associated protein 1 light chain 3; Mdivi-1: mitochondrial division inhibitor-1; MLD50: 50% mouse lethal dose; MOI: multiplicity of infection; MTOR: mechanistic target of rapamycin kinase; qPCR: quantitative real-time polymerase chain reaction; RABV: rabies virus; SQSTM1/p62: sequestosome 1; WT: wild type.

印度尼西亚东努沙登加拉省沃洛梅泽分区 Natarandang 社区卫生中心为被狗咬伤的患者注射狂犬病疫苗的情况

Profile of Rabies Vaccination Administration for Patients Bitten by Dogs at the Natarandang Community Health Centre, Wolomeze Sub-district, East Nusa Tenggara, Indonesia
作者： Sri Natalia Ginting
摘要中文翻译简介:狂犬病是一种病毒性疾病,一旦出现临床症状,几乎总是致命的,而狗是主要传播源。本研究旨在描述沃洛梅兹区 Natarandang 健康中心被狗咬伤的患者的狂犬病疫苗接种情况。方法:对 2022 年 1 月至 2023 年 2 月期间 32 名被狗咬伤后接受狂犬病疫苗接种的患者进行回顾性描述性研究。从病历中收集数据并进行描述性分析。结果:大多数患者为男性(65.6%),最常见的咬伤部位为小腿(34.4%)和手(28.1%)。最常见的急救措施包括检查狗的感染史和就医(各占34.4%)。最常用的狂犬病疫苗是 PCECV(81.3%)。延误医疗护理与发烧(65.6%)为主要症状有关。结论:Natarandang 健康中心的狗咬伤患者大多数为男性,最常见的咬伤部位是小腿和手部。最常报告的急救方法是检查狗的病史并寻求医疗救助。主要接种的狂犬病疫苗是 PCECV。发烧是最常见的症状。

问题与背景
狂犬病是一种病毒性疾病,一旦出现临床症状,几乎总是致命的,而狗是主要传播源。

对 2022 年 1 月至 2023 年 2 月期间被狗咬伤后接受狂犬病疫苗接种的 32 名患者进行的回顾性描述性研究。

主要结果
延误医疗护理与发烧(65.6%)为主要症状有关。

Profile of Rabies Vaccination Administration for Patients Bitten by Dogs at the Natarandang Community Health Centre, Wolomeze Sub-district, East Nusa Tenggara, Indonesia

Authors: Sri Natalia Ginting
Original Abstract
Introduction: Rabies is a viral disease that is almost always fatal once clinical symptoms appear, with dogs being the primary source of transmission. This study aims to describe the rabies vaccination profile of dog bite patients at Natarandang Health Center, Wolomeze District. Methods: A retrospective descriptive study on 32 patients who received rabies vaccination after dog bites between January 2022 and February 2023. Data were collected from medical records and analyzed descriptively. Results: Most patients were male (65.6%), with the most common bite locations in the calf (34.4%) and hand (28.1%). The most frequent first aid measures included checking the dog's infection history and seeking medical attention (34.4% each). The most commonly administered rabies vaccine was PCECV (81.3%). Delayed medical care was associated with fever (65.6%) as the predominant symptom. Conclusion: Most dog bite patients at Natarandang Health Center were male, with the most common bite locations in the calf and hand. The most frequently reported first aid were checking the dog’s history and seeking medical attention. The predominant rabies vaccine administered was PCECV. Fever was the most common symptom.

“用于神经回路高通量重建的条码狂犬病原位连接组学”数据集

Dataset for "Barcoded Rabies In Situ Connectomics for high-throughput reconstruction of neural circuits"
作者： Petr Znamenskiy, Alexander Becalick, Antonin Blot, Molly Strom
摘要中文翻译“用于高通量重建神经回路的条形码狂犬病原位连接组学”的数据集此存储库包含 A. Znamenskiy 的“用于高通量神经回路重建的条形码狂犬病原位连接组学”的预处理数据。重现分析所需的代码可在 https://github.com/znamlab/brisc 上找到。存档包含一个配置文件和 5 个文件。子文件夹:<code><strong>barcode_diversity_analysis</strong></code>:来自病毒和质粒文库的测序数据<code><strong>rabies_barcoding</strong></code>:串行双光子断层扫描和共焦数据<code><strong>becalia_iss_microscope</strong></code>:显微镜校准<code><strong>ccf_streamlines</strong></code>:来自 Allen CCF 流线的数据,用于将细胞位置转换为皮质平面图坐标<code><strong>becalia_rabies_barseq</strong></code>:<i>原位</i>测序数据<code><strong>barcode_diversity_analysis</strong></code>测序数据在<code><strong>collapsed_barcode</strong></code>子文件夹中提供,其中包含每个库的子文件夹。每个子文件夹包含一个文本文件,其中包含 UMI 计数数量和每个条形码的序列。<code><strong>rabies_barcoding</strong></code>PhP.eB 稀释实验和狂犬病批量标记的串行双光子断层扫描和共聚焦数据按每只小鼠的一个文件夹进行组织。以 <code><strong>BRJN</strong></code> 开头的小鼠是用于 PHP.eB 稀释的 Ai14 tdTomato 报告小鼠实验。<code><strong>mice_list.csv </strong></code>包含此实验的实验条件摘要。每个鼠标文件夹都包含一个 cellfinder_results 子文件夹,其中包含注册的 Allen CCF 图集(在注册文件夹中,25 um 体素分辨率)和检测到的细胞(在点文件夹中)。<code><strong>BRYC64.2h</strong></code> 是用于批量狂犬病追踪的鼠标(图 1 h-j)。它包含按上述方式组织的 cellfinder 数据以及 zeiss_confocal 文件夹中的示例共焦图像。<code><strong>tail_vs_local</strong></code>包含用于比较皮质内和静脉注射 Cre 的数据,包括两只小鼠注射部位的 tiff 文件以及 xml 文件中手动确定的细胞位置。<code><strong>becalia_iss_microscope</strong></code>包含显微镜在 <code><strong>becalia_iss_microscope/calibration/cellcam_dark_frame_2024_02_06/cellcam_dark_frame_2024_02_06_MMStack_Pos0.ome.tif</strong></code> 中包含暗帧图像,用于图像偏移估计。<code><strong>ccf_streamlines</strong></code>包含用于转换细胞的 Allen CCF 流线从 https://ccf-streamlines.readthedocs.io/en/latest/data_files.html 获取的皮质平面坐标位置。<code><strong>becalia_rabies_barseq</strong></code>包含原位测序数据。主要的原位测序位于 <code><strong>BRAC8498.3e</strong></code> 文件夹中。<code><strong>section_position.csv</strong></code> 中详细介绍了串行部分的位置。<code><strong>BRAC8498.3e/analysis</strong></code> 包含处理后的数据,包括:<code><strong>adata_q.h5ad</strong></code>:所有细胞基因表达的 AnnData 数据集<code><strong>BRAC8498.3e_error_ Corrected_barcodes_26_cell_barcode_df.pkl</strong></code>:包含所有细胞条形码信息的 pandas DataFrame cells<code><strong>BRAC8498.3e_error_ Corrected_barcodes_26_in_situ_barcodes.pkl</strong></code><b> </b>和<code><strong>BRAC8498.3e_error_ Corrected_barcodes_26_random_barcodes.pkl</strong></code>:包含质粒库与原位或随机检测到的条形码之间的匹配分别是条形码。它们可以使用 https://github.com/znamlab/brisc 中的 <code><strong>ma​​tch_to_library.py </strong></code> 重新生成。<code><strong>analysis/mcherry_cells</strong></code>:包含 的位置。手动检测到的非测序流动槽 (chamber_06) 的 mCherry 细胞<code><strong>analysis/starter_cells</strong></code>:包含带有起始细胞位置和荧光信息的 pandas DataFrame<code><strong>chamber_XX</strong></code>:各个文件夹中 4 个测序流动槽的数据,包含带有条形码点信息的 pandas DataFrame (<code><strong>barcode_round_spots_1.pkl</strong></code>)、检测到的 mCherry 细胞的位置 (<code><strong>cells/mCherry_1_cells/mCherry_1_df_ Corrected_curated.pkl</strong></code>) 以及每个 ROI 的 Allen CCF 注册 (<code><strong>register_to_ara</strong></code>),包括每个 ROI 的元数据文件ROI、多通道 tiff 文件形式的 CCF 中每个像素的 x、y、z 坐标 (<code><strong>ara_coordinates</strong></code>) 以及每个像素的区域 ID (<code><strong>area_images</strong></code>)。仅对于 <code><strong>chamber_08</strong></code>,来自一个图块的示例原始数据可以在 <code><strong>barcode_round_X_1</strong></code> 和 <code><strong>genes_round_X_1</strong></code> 文件夹中找到(其中 X 是测序轮数)。<code><strong>BRAC8780.3f</strong></code>:用于图 3e 敏感性分析的数据。它包含来自一个流动槽 (<code><strong>chamber_08</strong></code>) 的数据,包括杂交轮次的原始数据 (<code><strong>hybridization_round_1_1</strong></code>) 以及注册信息 (<code><strong>r</strong></code><code><strong>eg</strong></code>) 和元数据。

Dataset for "Barcoded Rabies In Situ Connectomics for high-throughput reconstruction of neural circuits"

Authors: Petr Znamenskiy, Alexander Becalick, Antonin Blot, Molly Strom
Original Abstract
Dataset for "Barcoded Rabies In Situ Connectomics for high-throughput reconstruction of neural circuits"This repository contains preprocessed data for "Barcoded Rabies In Situ Connectomics for high-throughput reconstruction of neural circuits" by A. Becalick, A. Blot, M. Strom, and P. Znamenskiy.The code required to reproduce the analyses is available at https://github.com/znamlab/brisc.The archive contains a config file and 5 subfolders:<code><strong>barcode_diversity_analysis</strong></code>: sequencing data from viral and plasmid libraries<code><strong>rabies_barcoding</strong></code>: serial two-photon tomography and confocal data<code><strong>becalia_iss_microscope</strong></code>: microscope calibration<code><strong>ccf_streamlines</strong></code>: data from Allen CCF streamlines for conversion of cell locations to cortical flatmap coordinates<code><strong>becalia_rabies_barseq</strong></code>: <i>in situ </i>sequencing data<code><strong>barcode_diversity_analysis</strong></code>The sequencing data are provided in the <code><strong>collapsed_barcode</strong></code> subfolder, which contains a subfolder for each library. Each subfolder contains one text file with number of UMI count and the sequence for each barcode.<code><strong>rabies_barcoding</strong></code>The serial two-photon tomography and confocal data for the PhP.eB dilution experiments and the rabies bulk labeling are organised with a folder per mouse.Mice starting with <code><strong>BRJN</strong></code> are the Ai14 tdTomato reporter mice used for the PHP.eB dilution experiments. <code><strong>mice_list.csv </strong></code>contains a summary of experimental conditions for this experiment. Each mouse folder contains a cellfinder_results subfolder with the registered Allen CCF atlas (at 25 um voxel resolution, in the registration folder) and the detected cells (in the points folder).<code><strong>BRYC64.2h</strong></code> is the mouse used for bulk rabies tracing (Fig1 h-j). It contains the cellfinder data, organised as described above and an example confocal image in the zeiss_confocal folder.<code><strong>tail_vs_local</strong></code> contains data for the comparison of intracortical and intravenous delivery of Cre, including tiff files of the injection site of the two mice and the manually determined position of cells in an xml file.<code><strong>becalia_iss_microscope</strong></code>Contains calibration data of the microscope including a dark frame image in <code><strong>becalia_iss_microscope/calibration/cellcam_dark_frame_2024_02_06/cellcam_dark_frame_2024_02_06_MMStack_Pos0.ome.tif</strong></code> for image offset estimation.<code><strong>ccf_streamlines</strong></code>Contains the Allen CCF streamlines used for conversion of cell locations to cortical flatmap coordinates obtained from https://ccf-streamlines.readthedocs.io/en/latest/data_files.html.<code><strong>becalia_rabies_barseq</strong></code>Contains the in situ sequencing data. The main in situ sequencing is in the <code><strong>BRAC8498.3e</strong></code> folder. Positions of the serial sections are detailed in <code><strong>section_position.csv</strong></code>. <code><strong>BRAC8498.3e/analysis</strong></code> contains the processed data including:<code><strong>adata_q.h5ad</strong></code>: an AnnData dataset of gene expression of all cells<code><strong>BRAC8498.3e_error_corrected_barcodes_26_cell_barcode_df.pkl</strong></code>: a pandas DataFrame containing barcode information for all cells<code><strong>BRAC8498.3e_error_corrected_barcodes_26_in_situ_barcodes.pkl</strong></code><b> </b>and <code><strong>BRAC8498.3e_error_corrected_barcodes_26_random_barcodes.pkl</strong></code>: contain the matches between the plasmid library and the barcode detected in situ or random barcodes, respectively. They can be regenerated using <code><strong>match_to_library.py </strong></code>in https://github.com/znamlab/brisc.<code><strong>analysis/mcherry_cells</strong></code>: contains the position of. manually detected mCherry cells for the non-sequenced flow cell (chamber_06)<code><strong>analysis/starter_cells</strong></code>: contains pandas DataFrames with position and fluorescence information of starter cells<code><strong>chamber_XX</strong></code>: data for the 4 sequenced flow cells in individual folders, containing a pandas DataFrame with the barcode spot information (<code><strong>barcode_round_spots_1.pkl</strong></code>), the positions of detected mCherry cells (<code><strong>cells/mCherry_1_cells/mCherry_1_df_corrected_curated.pkl</strong></code>) and registration to the Allen CCF for each ROI (<code><strong>register_to_ara</strong></code>), including a metadata file per ROI, the x,y,z coordinates of each pixel in the CCF as multichannel tiff file (<code><strong>ara_coordinates</strong></code>) and the area ID for each pixel (<code><strong>area_images</strong></code>). For <code><strong>chamber_08</strong></code> only, example raw data from one tile can be found in the <code><strong>barcode_round_X_1</strong></code> and <code><strong>genes_round_X_1</strong></code> folders (where X is the sequencing round number).<code><strong>BRAC8780.3f</strong></code>: data for the sensitivity analysis of Figure 3e. It contains data from one flow cell (<code><strong>chamber_08</strong></code>) including raw data of the hybridisation round (<code><strong>hybridisation_round_1_1</strong></code>) as well as registration information (<code><strong>r</strong></code><code><strong>eg</strong></code>) and metadata.

2026 年世界人畜共患病日:通过同一个健康采取社区行动是预防未来疫情爆发的基础

World Zoonoses Day 2026: Community Action Through One Health Is the Foundation for Preventing Future Outbreaks
作者： Dr. Nidhi Rawat, Dr. C. Sannat, Dr. Nitin E. Gade and Dr. Jasmeet Singh
摘要中文翻译世界人畜共患疾病日:全球呼吁集体责任 每年 7 月 6 日,国际社会都会庆祝世界人畜共患疾病日,以纪念预防医学史上具有里程碑意义的事件。1885 年的今天,路易斯·巴斯德成功为一名被狂犬病狗咬伤的小男孩注射了第一支狂犬病疫苗,证明科学创新可以预防世界上最可怕的传染病之一。一个多世纪后,这一成就的意义远远超出了狂犬病的范围。它象征着人类与人畜共患病——那些在人类、动物和环境之间传播的感染——的持续斗争。

World Zoonoses Day 2026: Community Action Through One Health Is the Foundation for Preventing Future Outbreaks

Authors: Dr. Nidhi Rawat, Dr. C. Sannat, Dr. Nitin E. Gade and Dr. Jasmeet Singh
Original Abstract
World Zoonoses Day: A Global Call for Collective Responsibility Every year on 6 July, the global community observes World Zoonoses Day to commemorate a landmark event in the history of preventive medicine. On this day in 1885, Louis Pasteur successfully administered the first rabies vaccine to a young boy bitten by a rabid dog, demonstrating that scientific innovation could prevent one of the world's most feared infectious diseases. More than a century later, the significance of this achievement extends far beyond rabies. It symbolizes humanity's continuing struggle against zoonotic diseases—those infections that circulate at the interface of humans, animals and the environment.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究 · E1

RVG功能化小胶质细胞膜包被的铈没食子酸金属有机框架用于多靶点阿尔茨海默病治疗

RVG-functionalized microglial membrane–coated cerium–gallic acid metal–organic frameworks for multi-target Alzheimer's therapy
作者： Fengmei Yang, Yuehua Chen, Yujiao Yan, Ruixin Zhao, Liran Deng, Die Tian, Qi Wang, Meng Xie, Yutong Chen
摘要中文翻译阿尔茨海默病(AD)是一种多因素神经退行性疾病,其特征是异常的β-淀粉样蛋白(Aβ)聚集、氧化应激、神经炎症和金属离子稳态破坏,这些限制了传统单靶点疗法的疗效。为了解决这些相互交织的病理过程,我们开发了一种基于铈没食子酸生物金属有机框架(CeGA-MOF)的多功能纳米治疗平台。可逆的 Ce3+/Ce4+ 氧化还原循环能够有效清除活性氧 (ROS),而没食子酸既可以作为有机配体,也可以作为金属螯合剂,抑制金属离子介导的 Aβ 聚集,减轻氧化应激相关的神经毒性,并具有内在的抗炎活性。为了增强体内稳定性和脑输送,CeGA-MOF 涂有小胶质细胞膜,并用狂犬病病毒糖蛋白 (RVG) 肽进行功能化,产生仿生纳米系统 CeGA-MOF/B/R。小胶质细胞膜提供免疫逃避和炎症引导的靶向,而 RVG 则促进血脑屏障穿透。在体外,CeGA-MOF/B/R 有效螯合 Cu2+、Zn2+ 和 Fe3+,抑制金属离子诱导的 Aβ 聚集,并保护神经元。在 APP/PS1 转基因小鼠中,它减少了大脑 Aβ,促进抗炎小胶质细胞极化,并改善学习和记忆,凸显了其作为协同 AD 治疗的仿生纳米平台的潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
阿尔茨海默病(AD)是一种多因素神经退行性疾病,其特征是异常的β-淀粉样蛋白(Aβ)聚集、氧化应激、神经炎症和金属离子稳态破坏,这些限制了传统单靶点疗法的疗效。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
所提供的证据没有明确说明核心实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
可逆的 Ce3+/Ce4+ 氧化还原循环能够有效清除活性氧 (ROS),而没食子酸既可以作为有机配体,也可以作为金属螯合剂,抑制金属离子介导的 Aβ 聚集,减轻氧化应激相关的神经毒性,并具有内在的抗炎活性。
解释与创新
在体外,CeGA-MOF/B/R 有效螯合 Cu2+、Zn2+ 和 Fe3+,抑制金属离子诱导的 Aβ 聚集,并保护神经元。
科研与公卫意义
在 APP/PS1 转基因小鼠中,它减少了大脑 Aβ,促进抗炎小胶质细胞极化,并改善学习和记忆,凸显了其作为协同 AD 治疗的仿生纳米平台的潜力。
局限与证据强度
小胶质细胞膜提供免疫逃避和炎症引导的靶向,而 RVG 则促进血脑屏障穿透。

RVG-functionalized microglial membrane–coated cerium–gallic acid metal–organic frameworks for multi-target Alzheimer's therapy

Authors: Fengmei Yang, Yuehua Chen, Yujiao Yan, Ruixin Zhao, Liran Deng, Die Tian, Qi Wang, Meng Xie, Yutong Chen
Original Abstract
Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a multifactorial neurodegenerative disorder characterized by aberrant β-amyloid (Aβ) aggregation, oxidative stress, neuroinflammation, and disrupted metal ion homeostasis, which limit the efficacy of conventional single-target therapies. To address these intertwined pathological processes, we developed a multifunctional nanotherapeutic platform based on a cerium-gallic acid bio-metal-organic framework (CeGA-MOF). Reversible Ce3+/Ce4+ redox cycling enables efficient reactive oxygen species (ROS) scavenging, while gallic acid serves as both an organic ligand and metal chelator, inhibiting metal ion-mediated Aβ aggregation and alleviating oxidative stress-associated neurotoxicity, along with its intrinsic anti-inflammatory activity. To enhance in vivo stability and brain delivery, CeGA-MOF was coated with microglial membranes and functionalized with rabies virus glycoprotein (RVG) peptide, yielding a biomimetic nanosystem, CeGA-MOF/B/R. The microglial membrane provides immune evasion and inflammation-guided targeting, while RVG facilitates blood-brain barrier penetration. In vitro, CeGA-MOF/B/R effectively chelates Cu2+, Zn2+, and Fe3+, suppresses metal ion-induced Aβ aggregation, and protects neurons. In APP/PS1 transgenic mice, it reduced cerebral Aβ, promoted anti-inflammatory microglial polarization, and improved learning and memory, highlighting its potential as a biomimetic nanoplatform for synergistic AD therapy.

B 常规重要学术文献 · 研究 · E1

巴西潘塔纳尔湿地马科动物中的甲病毒马达相关脑脊髓炎揭示了虫媒病毒在城郊环境中的循环

Alphavirus madariaga-associated encephalomyelitis in equines from the Brazilian Pantanal reveals arbovirus circulation in a peri-urban environment
作者： Luana Dias do Nascimento, Tarcísio Ávila Dos Santos, Tiago Baldin, Rachel Vieira Paes de Barros, Sayanne Luns Hatum de Almeida, Nathalia Assis Pereira, Luciano Nakazato, Jean-Paul Carrera, Carolina Argenta Pescador, Edson Moleta Colodel
摘要中文翻译马达里亚加甲病毒 (MADV) 是东部马脑炎病毒复合体的成员,是南美洲一种新兴的虫媒病毒,有关其在马群体中的发生和分子特征的信息有限。本研究报告了巴西马托格罗索州马科动物 MADV 的检测和遗传特征。对两匹患有急性神经系统疾病、临床病程大约为两天的马进行了评估。组织病理学检查显示非化脓性脑膜脑脊髓炎。使用 TaqMan RT-qPCR 检测对嗜神经病毒进行分子筛查,狂犬病病毒、EHV-1、WEEV、VEEV、WNV 和黄病毒呈阴性,而检测到 MADV,Ct 值为 27.9 和 31.4。部分 NS1 和 E1 基因片段被扩增并测序。基于这两个基因的系统发育分析一致地将名为 Santo Antonio 的毒株置于 MADV 基因型 III 进化枝内,并在 E1 分析中得到了强有力的支持。这些病例发生在圣安东尼奥杜莱韦尔热附近的城郊地区,距离库亚巴约 30 公里,凸显了森林环境与人类改造环境之间的潜在界面。这些发现提供了巴西中西部 MADV 循环及其与马科疾病关联的分子证据。我们的研究结果强化了马作为嗜神经性虫媒病毒监测哨兵在到达人类之前的作用。

Alphavirus madariaga-associated encephalomyelitis in equines from the Brazilian Pantanal reveals arbovirus circulation in a peri-urban environment

Authors: Luana Dias do Nascimento, Tarcísio Ávila Dos Santos, Tiago Baldin, Rachel Vieira Paes de Barros, Sayanne Luns Hatum de Almeida, Nathalia Assis Pereira, Luciano Nakazato, Jean-Paul Carrera, Carolina Argenta Pescador, Edson Moleta Colodel
Original Abstract
Alphavirus madariaga (MADV), a member of the eastern equine encephalitis virus complex, is an emerging arbovirus in South America, with limited information regarding its occurrence and molecular characterization in equine populations. This study reports the detection and genetic characterization of MADV in equines from the state of Mato Grosso, Brazil. Two horses presenting acute neurological disease with a clinical course of approximately two days were evaluated. Histopathological examination revealed nonsuppurative meningoencephalomyelitis. Molecular screening for neurotropic viruses using TaqMan RT-qPCR assays was negative for rabies virus, EHV-1, WEEV, VEEV, WNV, and flaviviruses, while MADV was detected with Ct values of 27.9 and 31.4. Partial NS1 and E1 gene fragments were amplified and sequenced. Phylogenetic analyses based on both genes consistently placed the strain, named Santo Antonio, within the MADV genotype III clade, with strong support in the E1 analysis. The cases occurred in a peri-urban area located near Santo Antônio do Leverger, approximately 30 km from Cuiabá, highlighting a potential interface between sylvatic and human-modified environments. These findings provide molecular evidence of MADV circulation in central-western Brazil and association with equines disease. Our results reinforce the role of horses as sentinels for neurotropic arbovirus surveillance before reaching human population.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述 · E2

基于机器学习的狂犬病暴露患者健康焦虑轨迹分析

Analysis of health anxiety trajectories in rabies exposure patients based on machine learning
作者： Yue’e Ma, Hua Xie, Xin Liu, Bin Lu, Minghe Jiang, Chenghang Mo, Hongbo Li, Fangzhu Li, Lan Liang, Guodong Xiong
摘要中文翻译目的:识别狂犬病初次暴露患者健康焦虑的动态演变轨迹,并利用机器学习算法对健康焦虑轨迹进行预测分析。方法:选取2024年1月至2025年10月期间南宁市某三级医院狂犬病暴露患者548例,按5剂次免疫方案,采用健康焦虑量表评估暴露后0、3、7、14、28天的健康焦虑水平。采用潜在变量增长混合模型来识别焦虑发展轨迹。利用6种机器学习算法建立高危健康焦虑识别模型,并对模型性能进行综合评价,并采用SHAP算法进行模型解释。结果:548 名患者全部完成了调查。潜变量增长混合模型确定了三种健康焦虑发展轨迹:低水平焦虑稳定组、中高水平焦虑改善组和高持续焦虑难以缓解组。多因素logistic回归分析显示,女性、大学本科及以上学历、狂犬病知识较高、合并症较多、III级暴露、延迟就医是“高持续性焦虑难以缓解组”的独立危险因素。相比之下,较高水平的健康素养被认为是一个保护因素。XGBoost 模型在独立测试集上表现出卓越的预测性能,受试者工作特征曲线下面积为 0.881,而 SHAP 分析确保了临床可解释性。XGBoost 模型的 SHAP 分析显示,健康素养、感知社会支持、狂犬病知识、支付方式和动物来源是预测“高持续性群体”的五个最重要特征。结论:狂犬病暴露患者的健康焦虑轨迹存在异质性。基于机器学习的预测模型有助于早期识别高危人群,为实施精准心理干预提供基础。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
识别初次暴露于狂犬病的患者健康焦虑的动态演变轨迹,并利用机器学习算法对健康焦虑轨迹进行预测分析。
证据基础与方法
曾孟思作为研究成员之一,由于作者数量较多,没有被列入作者名单。
共识与结论
结果 548名患者全部完成调查。
争议与缺口
结论 狂犬病暴露患者的健康焦虑轨迹存在异质性。
科研与实践启示
广西医科大学青年科学基金资助(GXMUYSF202455)。

Analysis of health anxiety trajectories in rabies exposure patients based on machine learning

Authors: Yue’e Ma, Hua Xie, Xin Liu, Bin Lu, Minghe Jiang, Chenghang Mo, Hongbo Li, Fangzhu Li, Lan Liang, Guodong Xiong
Original Abstract
OBJECTIVE: To identify the dynamic evolution trajectory of health anxiety in patients with initial rabies exposure and to conduct predictive analysis of health anxiety trajectories using machine learning algorithms. METHODS: A total of 548 rabies exposure patients from a tertiary hospital in Nanning were selected between January 2024 and October 2025. Health anxiety levels were assessed at 0, 3, 7, 14, and 28 days post-exposure using the Health Anxiety Scale following a 5-dose immunization schedule. A latent variable growth mixture model was employed to identify anxiety development trajectories. Six machine learning algorithms were utilized to establish a high-risk health anxiety identification model, and a comprehensive evaluation of the model's performance was conducted, with the SHAP algorithm used for model interpretation. RESULTS: All 548 patients completed the survey. The latent variable growth mixture model identified three health anxiety development trajectories: low-level anxiety-stable group, moderate to high-level anxiety-improving group, and high persistent anxiety-hard to relieve group. Multivariate logistic regression analysis indicated that being female, having a college degree or higher, high rabies knowledge, a greater number of comorbidities, level III exposure, and delayed medical treatment were independent risk factors belonging to the 'high persistent anxiety-hard to relieve group.' In contrast, a higher level of health literacy was identified as a protective factor. The XGBoost model demonstrated superior predictive performance on the independent test set, with an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.881, while SHAP analysis ensured clinical interpretability. SHAP analysis of the XGBoost model revealed that health literacy, Perceived social support, rabies knowledge, Payment method and Animal source were the five most important features predicting the 'high persistent group. CONCLUSION: There is heterogeneity in the health anxiety trajectories of patients exposed to rabies. A predictive model based on machine learning can facilitate the early identification of high-risk individuals, providing a basis for implementing precise psychological interventions.

非编码RNA在弹状病毒科病毒引起的感染中的调节作用

Regulatory role of noncoding RNAs in infections caused by Rhabdoviridae family viruses
作者： Fatemeh Zahra Mohammadzadeh, Anahita Nabavi, Mohammad Karimian, F. Mohammadzadeh
摘要中文翻译弹状病毒科由负义单链 RNA 病毒组成,可在多种宿主中引起严重感染。越来越多的证据表明,宿主非编码 RNA (ncRNA),包括 microRNA (miRNA)、长链非编码 RNA (lncRNA) 和环状 RNA (circRNA),在调节弹状病毒复制、发病机制和宿主免疫反应中发挥着核心作用。这些 ncRNA 通过调节先天免疫信号通路、基因表达的调节和炎症反应的控制来发挥抗病毒和前病毒作用。抗病毒 ncRNA 通过促进干扰素产生和干扰素刺激基因以及激活炎症和抗病毒防御途径来抑制病毒复制。另一方面,前病毒 ncRNA 通过调节或抑制 NF-κB 和 JAK/STAT 等关键信号通路来促进病毒存活和复制。这篇综述提供了弹状病毒感染中 ncRNA 介导的调控机制的以通路为中心的综合,特别强调了金迪普拉病毒、水泡性口炎病毒和狂犬病病毒。通过整合目前关于 ncRNA 和弹状病毒之间细胞和分子相互作用的研究结果,这篇综述强调了 ncRNA 在病毒发病机制和宿主免疫中的双重调节作用,并讨论了它们作为控制弹状病毒感染的治疗靶点和生物标志物的潜力。

争议与缺口

科研与实践启示

Regulatory role of noncoding RNAs in infections caused by Rhabdoviridae family viruses

Authors: Fatemeh Zahra Mohammadzadeh, Anahita Nabavi, Mohammad Karimian, F. Mohammadzadeh
Original Abstract
The Rhabdoviridae family comprises negative-sense single-stranded RNA viruses that cause severe infections in a wide range of hosts. Increasing evidence indicates that host noncoding RNAs (ncRNAs), including microRNAs (miRNAs), long noncoding RNAs (lncRNAs), and circular RNAs (circRNAs), play central roles in regulating rhabdovirus replication, pathogenesis, and host immune responses. These ncRNAs exert both antiviral and proviral effects through modulation of innate immune signaling pathways, regulation of gene expression, and control of inflammatory responses. Antiviral ncRNAs suppress viral replication by promoting interferon production and interferon-stimulated genes and by activating inflammatory and antiviral defense pathways. On the other hand, proviral ncRNAs facilitate viral survival and replication through modulation or inhibition of key signaling pathways such as NF-κB and JAK/STAT. This review provides a pathway-centric synthesis of ncRNA-mediated regulatory mechanisms in rhabdovirus infections, with particular emphasis on Chandipura virus, vesicular stomatitis virus, and rabies virus. By integrating current findings on the cellular and molecular interactions between ncRNAs and rhabdoviruses, this review highlights the dual regulatory roles of ncRNAs in viral pathogenesis and host immunity and discusses their potential as therapeutic targets and biomarkers for the control of rhabdoviral infections.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述 · E1

狂犬病和新出现的感染:在新的溢出时代对最古老的人畜共患疾病进行一次健康审查

Rabies and emerging infections: a one health review of the oldest zoonosis in an age of new spillovers
作者： Mervin Kennady R.V.,Thirumurthi K., Veeramalla Divya, Dimalie Michui
摘要中文翻译1885 年 7 月 6 日,路易斯·巴斯德 (Louis Pasteur) 为一名被咬伤的儿童接种了第一支狂犬病疫苗,这一时刻现在被定为世界人畜共患病日来纪念。近一个半世纪后,狂犬病仍然是医学上已知的最致命的人畜共患疾病,尽管疫苗完全可以预防,但每年仍夺去约五万九千人的生命。这篇综述以狂犬病为镜头,审视动物-人类-环境界面上出现和重新出现的感染的更广泛情况。我们追踪狂犬病病毒及其狂犬病毒属近亲的生物学、病毒侵入神经系统的机制以及定义该疾病的临床和实验室特征。然后,我们总结了全球和印度的负担、现代预防支柱——伤口护理、暴露后预防、大规模狗疫苗接种、口服疫苗的新兴使用和综合监测——以及印度通过其消除狗介导的狂犬病国家行动计划所采用的世界卫生组织“零到30”目标。转向外部,我们询问为什么大多数新出现的人类病原体起源于动物,并通过包括高致病性禽流感 H5N1、尼帕病毒、SARS-CoV-2 和mpox 在内的详细例子来考虑溢出的生态和社会经济驱动因素。自始至终,我们认为“同一个健康”框架是将消除一种古老疾病与为新的疫情爆发做好准备联系起来的结缔组织,而处于这一界面的兽医对于这两项努力都是不可或缺的。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
这篇综述以狂犬病为镜头,审视动物-人类-环境界面上出现和重新出现的感染的更广泛情况。
证据基础与方法
提供的证据没有明确说明审查方法、数据库、资格流程或证据基础。
共识与结论
自始至终,我们认为“同一个健康”框架是将消除一种古老疾病与为新的疫情爆发做好准备联系起来的结缔组织,而处于这一界面的兽医对于这两项努力都是不可或缺的。
争议与缺口
转向外部,我们询问为什么大多数新出现的人类病原体起源于动物,并通过包括高致病性禽流感 H5N1、尼帕病毒、SARS-CoV-2 和mpox 在内的详细例子来考虑溢出的生态和社会经济驱动因素。
科研与实践启示
然后,我们总结了全球和印度的负担、现代预防支柱——伤口护理、暴露后预防、大规模狗疫苗接种、口服疫苗的新兴使用和综合监测——以及印度通过其消除狗介导的狂犬病国家行动计划所采用的世界卫生组织“零到30”目标。

Rabies and emerging infections: a one health review of the oldest zoonosis in an age of new spillovers

Authors: Mervin Kennady R.V.,Thirumurthi K., Veeramalla Divya, Dimalie Michui
Original Abstract
On 6 July 1885, Louis Pasteur administered the first rabies vaccine to a bitten child, a moment now commemorated annually as World Zoonoses Day. Almost a century and a half later, rabies endures as the most lethal zoonosis known to medicine, claiming an estimated fifty-nine thousand human lives every year despite being entirely vaccine-preventable. This review uses rabies as a lens through which to examine the broader landscape of emerging and re-emerging infections that arise at the animal–human–environment interface. We trace the biology of the rabies virus and its relatives within the genus Lyssavirus, the mechanisms by which the virus invades the nervous system, and the clinical and laboratory features that define the disease. We then summarise the global and Indian burden, the modern pillars of prevention—wound care, post-exposure prophylaxis, mass dog vaccination, the emerging use of oral vaccines and integrated surveillance—and the World Health Organization’s “Zero by 30” goal that India has adopted through its National Action Plan for Dog Mediated Rabies Elimination. Turning outward, we ask why the majority of newly emerging human pathogens originate in animals, and we consider the ecological and socio-economic drivers of spillover through detailed examples including highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1, Nipah virus, SARS-CoV-2 and mpox. Throughout, we argue that the One Health framework is the connective tissue linking the elimination of an ancient disease to preparedness for novel outbreaks, and that veterinarians, positioned at this interface, are indispensable to both endeavours.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述 · E2

对 2005-2025 年“同一个健康”干预措施的范围审查:已经做了哪些工作,这些措施是否有效,以及最佳实践是什么?

A scoping review of One Health interventions 2005–2025: What has been done, are they effective, and what are the best practices?
作者： Eri Togami, Lincoln Hopkins, Lori Rosman, Meghan F. Davis, Meghan F Davis
摘要中文翻译背景:“同一个健康”研究和实践正在迅速发展,需要对“同一个健康”干预措施建立可靠的证据和质量评估。我们进行了范围界定审查,目的是 (1) 描述同行评审文献中发表的 One Health 干预措施的数量和类型;(2) 评估所确定的干预措施是否有效。我们假设证据的数量和证明有效性的能力有限。方法:根据系统评价的首选报告项目和范围界定评价的荟萃分析扩展来报告评价。最初的检索于 2024 年 5 月在 Medline (Ovid)、Embase、PubAg 和 Web of Science 上进行,并于 2025 年 12 月更新。纳入标准为: 研究人员干预人类、动物或其共享环境(三个领域中的至少两个)的主要研究;其中包括结果评估,全文有英文版,于 2025 年 12 月 16 日或之前发表。两名研究人员独立筛选和分析了所有手稿。结果:筛选了超过 18,000 篇参考文献,纳入了 151 项研究。研究在代表世卫组织所有六个区域的 53 个国家进行。据报告,美国、坦桑尼亚和印度的干预次数最多。在 79 项针对人畜共患疾病的干预措施中,最常见的疾病是一般人畜共患病、狂犬病和囊虫病。超过一半 (55%) 的研究设计是准实验性的,其次是 23% 的随机对照试验 (RCT) 和 19% 的非随机实验。不到一半 (41%) 的研究设有对照组。最常见的偏见来源是移民(28%)和移民选择偏见(11%)。八项基于现场的随机对照试验表明,与对照相比,“同一个健康”干预措施可有效减少疾病发生或病原体检测。典型的实践包括跨越人类、动物和环境领域的综合干预,使用参与式方法,评估长期结果,使用多种方法对结果进行三角测量,不夸大发现,并提供视觉辅助来描述干预措施。结论:少数实地随机对照试验清楚地证明了“同一个健康”干预措施的相对有效性,而在没有更细粒度的有效性定义的情况下,评估其他研究的有效性具有挑战性。评估“同一个健康”干预措施有效性的严格研究将共同​​提高政府、研究人员和政策制定者的支持度,并推进采用“同一个健康”方法的证据基础。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述审查了人类、动物和环境领域的“同一个健康”干预措施,重点关注 2005 年至 2025 年同行评审文献中的特征和有效性评估。核心问题是存在哪些类型的“同一个健康”干预措施,以及它们是否在减少疾病发生或病原体检测方面表现出有效性。
证据基础与方法
本次范围界定审查遵循 PRISMA-ScR 指南,并于 2024 年 5 月检索了 Medline (Ovid)、Embase、PubAg 和 Web of Science,并于 2025 年 12 月更新。纳入标准要求进行初步研究,干预措施至少涵盖三个 One Health 领域中的两个、结果评估和英文全文,于 2025 年 12 月 16 日出版。两名独立研究人员筛选和分析了手稿。
共识与结论
八项实地随机对照试验 (RCT) 表明,与对照相比,One Health 干预措施有效减少了疾病发生或病原体检测。典型的做法包括全面的多领域干预措施、参与性方法、长期结果评估、方法三角测量、对结果的谨慎解释以及描述干预措施的视觉辅助工具。
争议与缺口
不到一半 (41%) 的研究包括对照组或比较组,并且准实验设计占主导地位 (55%),限制了因果推断。选择偏差,特别是移民(28%)和移民(11%),经常被发现。该审查强调缺乏有效性的细化定义,使实地随机对照试验之外的评估变得复杂。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应优先考虑与对照组进行严格的研究设计,以加强因果证据。标准化有效性定义并纳入长期结果和三角测量方法至关重要。解决选择偏差和扩大多领域干预措施可以增强政策相关性和利益相关者的支持。

A scoping review of One Health interventions 2005–2025: What has been done, are they effective, and what are the best practices?

Authors: Eri Togami, Lincoln Hopkins, Lori Rosman, Meghan F. Davis, Meghan F Davis
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: One Health research and practice are evolving rapidly, necessitating solid evidence-building and quality assessment of One Health interventions. We conducted a scoping review to (1) characterize how many and what kinds of One Health interventions are published in peer-reviewed literature; and (2) assess whether the identified interventions demonstrated effectiveness. We hypothesized that the evidence was limited in numbers and ability to demonstrate effectiveness. METHODS: The review was reported according to the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses extension for Scoping Reviews. The initial search was conducted on Medline (Ovid), Embase, PubAg, and Web of Science in May 2024 and updated in December 2025. Inclusion criteria were: Primary research in which the investigators intervened with humans, animals, or their shared environment (at least two of three domains); an outcome assessment was included, and full text was available in English, published on or before December 16, 2025. Two researchers independently screened and analyzed all manuscripts. RESULTS: Over 18,000 references were screened, and 151 studies were included. Studies were conducted in 53 countries representing all six WHO Regions. The highest number of interventions were reported from the United States of America, Tanzania, and India. Among 79 interventions that targeted zoonoses, the most common diseases were general zoonoses, rabies, and cysticercosis. Over half (55%) of the study designs were quasi-experimental, followed by 23% randomized control trials (RCTs) and 19% non-randomized experiments. Less than half (41%) of the studies had a control or comparison group. The most common perceived sources of bias were immigrative (28%) and emigrative selection bias (11%). Eight field-based RCTs identified that a One Health intervention was effective in reducing disease occurrence or pathogen detection compared to the control. Exemplary practices included a comprehensive intervention spanning human, animal, and environmental domains, using a participatory approach, assessing longer-term outcomes, triangulating results using more than one method, not overstating findings, and providing a visual aid to describe the intervention. CONCLUSIONS: A small number of field-based RCTs clearly demonstrated the comparative effectiveness of One Health interventions, while it was challenging to assess the effectiveness of other studies without a more granular definition of effectiveness. Rigorous studies that assess the effectiveness of One Health interventions will collectively improve buy-in from governments, researchers, and policymakers, and advance the evidence base for employing the One Health approach.

女子称人工智能视觉搜索帮助她意识到自己被蝙蝠咬伤,需要注射狂犬病疫苗

Woman says AI visual search helped her realize she’d been bitten by a bat and needed a rabies shot
正文要点中文精炼2026 年 5 月,一名 53 岁的英国妇女在希腊莱斯博斯岛度假,使用 Google 镜头识别了她从游泳池救出的一只蝙蝠。人工智能工具通过突出显示她没有感觉到的咬痕来提醒她可能接触狂犬病,并促使她立即就医。由于语言障碍,初始治疗被推迟后,她接受了四剂狂犬病暴露后预防 (PEP)。该事件强调了蝙蝠遭遇的风险以及人工智能工具在提高人畜共患危害意识方面的作用。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:世界标准时间 2026 年 7 月 15 日 16:41。事件发生在 2026 年 5 月的两周假期期间。
地点与对象
英国(英格兰利物浦)和希腊(莱斯博斯岛)。散客(53岁,女性)。
事件
一名妇女使用人工智能视觉搜索工具(Google Lens)识别了她救出的一只蝙蝠,在发现她没有感觉到的咬痕后,促使她寻求狂犬病暴露后预防 (PEP)。
规模、影响与风险
没有确诊狂犬病感染。个人暴露于蝙蝠咬伤。需要四剂狂犬病 PEP。明确指出了蝙蝠接触传播狂犬病的风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
医疗咨询确认需要 PEP。最初的语言障碍延迟了治疗。完成完整的 PEP 课程。来源是媒体报道的个人账户。

Woman Says AI Visual Search Helped Her Realize She’d Been Bitten by a Bat and Needed a Rabies Shot “It was lucky Google Lens told me to go to hospital — who knows what might have happened?” Laura Horton said By Vanessa Etienne Vanessa Etienne Vanessa Etienne is a Staff Writer for PEOPLE on the Health team. She joined the brand in 2021. People Editorial Guidelines Published on July 15, 2026 04:41PM EDT 1 Comment Laura Horton and the bat she found. Credit : Laura Horton/SWNS NEED TO KNOW Laura Horton used Google Lens to identify a bat she rescued and discovered potential rabies exposureThe AI tool prompted her to seek medical attention after noticing bite marks she hadn’t feltHorton hopes sharing her story raises awareness about rabies risks from wild animal encounters A woman is praising an AI tool for helping her seek life-saving treatment. In May 2026, Laura Horton — a 53-year-old personal trainer from Liverpool, England — was celebrating her birthday with a two-week vacation to Lesbos, Greece. On the fourth day of the trip, she noticed a bat was drowning in the swimming pool at the apartment complex she was staying in. Not thinking much of it, she decided to rescue the animal. “I grabbed it from the water on instinct because it was drowning — I had no idea of the risks,” she told Southwest News Service. “It sat on my hand for about 10 minutes, drying off.” “We all thought it looked cute, so I took a photo of it,” she added. The villa where Laura was bitten. Laura Horton/SWNS Our new app is here! Free, fun and full of exclusives. Scan to download now! After the bat flew away, Horton said she uploaded the photo to Google Lens — an AI tool that allows users to search what they see using their device’s camera — to figure out what species of bat it was. However, the results that came up were far from expected. “It started asking questions like, ‘Are those your hands?’ and ‘Have you washed your hands for 15 minutes after handling it?’” she recalled. “It warned me of the risk of rabies and said I wouldn’t feel a bite, as their teeth are too small.” “I thought it was all a bit dramatic,” she admitted. “But when I took a photo of my hand, I saw two tiny bite marks. I had no idea you could catch rabies from a bat.” Laura Horton holding the bat. Laura Horton/SWNS Related Stories Rabies Is Making a Comeback. Should You Be Worried? Organ Donor Unknowingly Gave Rabies to Cornea Graft Recipient as Well as Kidney Recipient Who Died Horton decided to call her healthcare provider the following day, which confirmed that she should seek medical attention. She was informed that she would need to receive the rabies post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) to prevent the virus from reaching her nervous system. She immediately went to a local hospital, but claimed that due to the language barrier, getting proper treatment was difficult. “At first, they tried to send me away with antibiotics. I’m not sure they understood what happened,” she said. “I was sent to a travel clinic, and a nurse was furious I hadn’t been given the first dose yet. I went back to the hospital and found the infectious diseases department – they were brilliant.” Bite marks on Laura Horton’s hand. Laura Horton/SWNS Sign up for From the Editor, our free weekly newsletter from PEOPLE’s Editor-in-Chief, Charlotte Triggs. While in Greece, Horton received two more shots before returning home on June 1 and receiving a fourth dose with her own doctor. “It was lucky Google Lens told me to go to hospital – who knows what might have happened,” she told the outlet. Laura Horton on vacation. Laura Horton/SWNS Never miss a story — sign up for PEOPLE’s free daily newsletter to stay up-to-date on the best of what PEOPLE has to offer​​, from celebrity news to compelling human interest stories. Horton said she’s been researching more about cases of rabies and is hoping that sharing her story will help others stay safe. “I read the tragic case about the boy in Canada who died after a bat landed on his face,” she said. “A lot of people aren’t aware of the dangers of these wild animals and the risks. Hopefully I can help raise awareness.” Laura Horton on vacation. Laura Horton/SWNS According to the Mayo Clinic, there’s no immediate sign that you’ve been infected with rabies, the deadly virus that’s nearly always spread to humans from contact with an infected animal. This is why doctors advise people to seek medical treatment immediately after any contact with a wild animal, and to undergo a series of rabies vaccines. Once symptoms begin to show, the condition is usually fatal. The incubation period depends on how far away from the brain the bite or scratch is, and how severe it is — and/or how much of the virus is transferred, the CDC warns. 蒙大拿州官员警告宠物主人保持警惕,因为今年夏天狂犬病病例上升 - KTVH

Montana Officials Warn Pet Owners To Stay Alert As Rabies Cases Rise This Summer - KTVH
正文要点中文精炼蒙大拿州畜牧官员今年夏天警告居民野生动物狂犬病活动增加,特别是全州范围内的蝙蝠和东南部的臭鼬。截至 2026 年 7 月中旬,黄石县、加勒廷县和弗拉特黑德县已确认存在三只患有狂犬病的蝙蝠。当局报告称,每天都有多个电话涉及宠物或人类接触可能受感染的野生动物的情况,其中人类接触往往与室内发现的蝙蝠有关。官员们强调,狂犬病并不总是会引起动物的攻击行为,并建议对任何咬伤或接触立即就医。家畜疫苗接种被认为是最有效的预防措施。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表日期:2026-07-15。事件日期:2026年夏季(报告时间为2026年7月14日至15日)。
地点与对象
受影响的人群包括宠物、牲畜和接触野生动物的人类。
事件
蒙大拿州畜牧部官员就今年夏天野生动物中狂犬病病例的增加发出公开警告,强调全州范围内的蝙蝠和蒙大拿州东南部的臭鼬。该警告是基于已确认的患有狂犬病的动物和报告的暴露情况。
规模、影响与风险
黄石县、加拉廷县和弗拉特黑德县报告了三只确诊的狂犬病蝙蝠。每天多次接到有关宠物或人类接触野生动物的电话。通过咬伤或接触将狂犬病传播给家畜和人类的风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
官员建议如果被咬伤或接触,应立即就医。强调对宠物和牲畜进行疫苗接种作为一级预防。鼓励公开举报疑似病例。没有提到具体的检疫或扑杀措施。

Montana Officials Warn Pet Owners To Stay Alert As Rabies Cases Rise This Summer - KTVH

Fetched Original Body
News Actions Facebook Tweet Email Montana Officials Warn Pet Owners To Stay Alert As Rabies Cases Rise This Summer Prev Next By: Logan Cyr Posted 10:58 PM, Jul 14, 2026 HELENA — Montana livestock officials are warning residents to stay alert for possible rabies exposure this summer as reports involving bats and other wildlife continue across the state. The Montana Department of Livestock says bats are the most common source of rabies cases in Montana, while skunks are typically linked to cases in southeastern parts of the state. 1/1 Skip Ad Continue watching after the ad Visit Advertiser website GO TO PAGE “So in Montana the 2 types of wildlife that we see with rabies are skunks in the southeastern part of the state and then bats statewide,” said Dr. Emily Kaleczyc, assistant state veterinarian with the Department of Livestock. State officials say there have been three confirmed rabid bats in Montana so far this year, including cases in Yellowstone, Gallatin and Flathead counties. The department also says it receives several calls a day involving pets or people exposed to wildlife. “A lot of human exposures, especially in Montana, are related to people finding bats in homes or cabins,” Kaleczyc said. Officials say anyone who may have been bitten or exposed to a bat should contact a doctor or public health official right away. They also warn that rabies does not always cause aggressive behavior in animals. Pets or livestock infected with rabies may simply act differently than normal. “Any changes in behavior, especially anything neurologic related, can be a sign of rabies in domestic species,” Kaleczyc said. State veterinarians say keeping pets and livestock vaccinated is the best way to prevent the disease. “So we have very effective vaccines against rabies for all of our domestic species, dogs, cats, all of our livestock species,” Kaleczyc said. Officials encourage anyone with questions or concerns about possible rabies exposure to contact the Montana Department of Livestock at 406-444-2976 or visit the department’s website. B 常规重要

在海狗身上发现的狂犬病凸显了狂犬病生态的前所未有的转变 - WOAH

Rabies detected in Cape fur seals highlights an unprecedented shift in rabies ecology - WOAH
正文要点中文精炼世界动物卫生组织 (WOAH) 报告称,前所未有地发现南部非洲海狗持续传播狂犬病。这一发展标志着狂犬病生态学的重大转变,引发了有关野生动物健康的重要问题。该组织强调需要进一步调查和采取行动来应对这一新出现的威胁。所提供的证据中并未详细说明影响和应对措施的全部范围,但 WOAH 的声明强调了监测和控制野生动物种群中的狂犬病以防止更广泛的健康风险的重要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布时间:2026年07月17日,事件日期未在所提供的证据中报告。
地点与对象
南部非洲的海狗。
事件
WOAH 报告称,检测到海狗体内存在持续狂犬病传播。
规模、影响与风险
所提供的证据中未报告。
应对、状态与不确定性
WOAH 强调了狂犬病生态学前所未有的转变,提出了野生动物健康的重要问题。所提供的证据中未报告影响和反应的全部范围。

Founded in 1924 as the Office International des Epizooties (OIE), in May 2003 we adopted the common name World Organisation for Animal Health. An intergovernmental organisation, we focus on transparently disseminating information on animal diseases, improving animal health globally and thus build a safer, healthier and more sustainable world Mission Advocacy Strategy Structure and GovernanceToggle submenu Back to menu World Assembly Council Director General Governance Review Committee Organisation’s Framework Members Collaborate with us What we doToggle submenu Back to menuWhat we doWe work to improve animal health and welfare across the globe. By collecting, analysing and disseminating veterinary scientific information, we encourage international solidarity in the control of animal health risks. Furthermore, we work across borders to foster a One Health approach, recognising that the health of animals, humans and the environment are interdependent. Animal Health and WelfareToggle submenu Back to menu Animal Diseases Disease Data Collection Animal Welfare Wildlife Health Official Disease Status StandardsToggle submenu Back to menu Codes and Manuals Standard-Setting Process Observatory Global InitiativesToggle submenu Back to menu Disease Eradication Antimicrobial resistance One Health Biological threat reduction Food Safety Publications What we offerToggle submenu Back to menuWhat we offerWe oversee various programmes, both cross-sectoral ones and those focused on specific needs, to improve the capacities of Veterinary Services and Aquatic Animal Health Services. These programmes are designed to empower and guide countries in the development and implementation of sustainable national plans which go well beyond purely veterinary matters. Nipah and Hendra virus diseases Working across borders to foster a One Health approach to global health. The health of animals and the environment strongly depend on human activities. See more One Health The “One Health” approach summarises a concept that has been known for more than a century; that human, animal and plant health are interdependent and bound to the health of the ecosystems in which they exist. We envisage and implement it as a collaborative, whole of society, whole of government approach to understanding, anticipating and addressing risks to global health. Discover Antimicrobial resistance Imagine a world where infections and diseases in humans, animals and plants are impossible to treat. This worst-case scenario could become a reality as bacteria, viruses and parasites develop resistance to the drugs we use to fight them. Antimicrobial resistance, or AMR, has become one of the most pressing health issues of our time. Solutions exist and everyone has a role to play in the fight against this global threat. Discover Disease Data Collection The World Animal Health Information System (WAHIS) is the global reference platform for the publication of official data on epidemiologically important diseases in domestic and wild animals. The data tracks the evolution of diseases, assists in trade and policy decisions, and informs research. Discover Animal Welfare Animal welfare is a complex and multi-faceted subject with scientific, ethical, economic, cultural, social, religious and political dimensions. It is attracting growing interest from civil society and is one of the priorities of the World Organisation for Animal Health (WOAH). Discover Aquatic animals Aquatic animals play a key role in achieving a more prosperous and secure world, contributing to Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs). B 常规重要

黄石县报告确诊狂犬病病例,官员强调宠物疫苗接种 - KTVQ

Yellowstone County reports confirmed rabies case, officials stress pet vaccinations - KTVQ
正文要点中文精炼蒙大拿州卫生官员于 2026 年 7 月确认黄石县出现一例狂犬病病例,这是该县今年首次发现狂犬病病例,也是全州第三例。蒙大拿州畜牧部报告了这一病例,当地兽医敦促居民为宠物接种疫苗,并避免接触野生动物,特别是蝙蝠、臭鼬、浣熊和狐狸。一旦出现症状,狂犬病几乎总是致命的,并且测试需要动物死亡。当局强调预防至关重要,并指出自 2025 年 7 月以来蒙大拿州已确诊 27 例狂犬病,全部涉及蝙蝠。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表日期:2026-07-13。事件日期:2026年7月(当月较早时间)。时间范围:所提供证据中未报告。
地点与对象
美国蒙大拿州黄石县。受影响人群包括当地居民和宠物主人。
事件
据 KTVQ 和当地兽医报告,蒙大拿州畜牧部确认黄石县出现一例狂犬病病例。该事件已确认。
规模、影响与风险
2026 年,黄石县出现第一例狂犬病确诊病例。2026 年,全州报告第三例狂犬病病例。一旦出现症状,狂犬病几乎总是致命的。所提供的证据中没有报告人类病例。风险:可能接触携带狂犬病的野生动物。
应对、状态与不确定性
官员强调宠物疫苗接种和避免接触野生动物。测试需要死去的动物。调查正在进行中。所提供的证据中没有报告有关接触者追踪或人体暴露的更多详细信息。

Closed Captioning Information Manage Emails Apps Careers Search Q2News/ ktvq UCN2IWwjVxh4vOnMbnjiM8Hw News Montana Politics Montana Ag Network America 250 Your Health Matters More + Indian Country Positively Montana My MTN Contests Quick Links + News Montana Politics Montana Ag Network America 250 Your Health Matters Indian Country Positively Montana My MTN Contests News Actions Facebook Tweet Email Yellowstone County reports confirmed rabies case, officials stress pet vaccinations Prev Next Photo by: Facebook Post from Billings Animal Family Hospital By: Isabel Spartz Posted 3:04 AM, Jul 13, 2026 and last updated 4:02 PM, Jul 13, 2026 BILLINGS — State officials have confirmed a case of rabies in Yellowstone County, prompting veterinarians to remind residents to avoid contact with wildlife and ensure pets are current on their rabies vaccinations.The Montana Department of Livestock confirmed the case earlier this month. It is the first confirmed case in Yellowstone County this year and the third reported statewide in 2026.Watch the story here: Yellowstone County reports confirmed rabies case, officials stress pet vaccinations 1/1 Skip Ad Continue watchingafter the adVisit Advertiser websiteGO TO PAGE Dr. Edie Best, owner and lead veterinarian at Billings Animal Family Hospital, said while rabies cases remain uncommon, the disease is nearly always fatal once symptoms develop, making prevention critical."Rabies is 100% fatal," Best said. "The one vaccination that is absolutely mandatory is the rabies vaccination."Rabies is a viral disease that affects the nervous system and can spread to both animals and humans through bites or scratches from infected animals. Health officials say residents should never approach or handle wild animals, particularly species known to carry the virus, including bats, skunks, raccoons, and foxes.Best said unusual behavior is often the first warning sign."If you see one of those animals out in the daytime or acting unusually, like a bat that's laying on the ground or a skunk that's out in the middle of the day, ... stay away," she said. "That's not normal."While human rabies infections are rare, any potential exposure should be treated as a medical emergency."If you get bit by that animal, that's an emergency room trip immediately," Best said. "The hard part about testing for rabies is the only way to test is to test the brain, so the animal has to be deceased."According to the Montana Department of Livestock, there have been 27 confirmed rabies detections in Montana since July 2025, all involving bats. The Yellowstone County case marks the county's first confirmed detection this year.Best said residents shouldn't panic but should remain aware of their surroundings, especially when spending time outdoors."We've always known it's out there," she said. "It's just a heads up. If you see something that really seems unusual, especially in one of those animals, it's probably not normal." Source: Межа. Новини України.  |  Published: 2026-07-18  |  Fetched: 2026-07-18T19:07:50+00:00  |  Body: full / playwright:trafilatura_html2txt
B 常规重要

格鲁吉亚和世卫组织向乌克兰-Межа运送了2000瓶狂犬病免疫球蛋白。Новини України。

Georgia and WHO deliver 2,000 rabies immunoglobulin vials to Ukraine - Межа. Новини України.
正文要点中文精炼2026 年 7 月 18 日,乌克兰从格鲁吉亚和世界卫生组织收到了 2000 瓶狂犬病免疫球蛋白,用于紧急区域分发。这批货物支持全国指定抗狂犬病护理中心对高危患者进行暴露后预防。2026 年 5 月,已向区域中心分发了超过 42,000 剂狂犬病疫苗,2026 年第一季度,顿涅茨克有超过 260 人因动物咬伤而寻求治疗。卫生当局强调,狂犬病免疫球蛋白只适用于严重或多次咬伤、头部或颈部咬伤或粘膜暴露,医生根据个人风险评估做出治疗决定。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026 年 7 月 18 日,07:14(欧洲中部时间)。活动日期:2026年7月18日(交割日)。
地点与对象
乌克兰(区域分布)。来自格鲁吉亚和世界卫生组织的捐助者支持。
事件
向乌克兰运送 2,000 瓶狂犬病免疫球蛋白,以便紧急分发给抗狂犬病护理中心。乌克兰新闻社和卫生部报道。
规模、影响与风险
交付了 2,000 瓶狂犬病免疫球蛋白。支持高危患者的暴露后预防。2026 年 5 月,向各地区分发了超过 42,000 剂狂犬病疫苗。2026 年第一季度,顿涅茨克有超过 260 人因动物咬伤而寻求治疗。狂犬病风险上升,部分原因是军事行动。
应对、状态与不确定性
免疫球蛋白将分发到指定的区域狂犬病护理中心。中心根据需要提供医疗护理、初步治疗和抗狂犬病疫苗接种。预防方案由医生根据风险评估确定。所提供的证据中没有报告未解决的信息。

18:26 Maguchih claims silver in London Diamond League high jump 18 jul 2026, 18:24 Jensen Huang’s signed leather jacket fetches $960,000 at Sotheby’s Georgia and WHO deliver 2,000 rabies immunoglobulin vials to Ukraine Saturday, 18 July 2026, 07:14 A fresh shipment of 2,000 rabies immunoglobulin vials arrived for urgent regional distribution. Health officials say the supply will support post-exposure treatment for high-risk patients. As reported by the online media Ukrinform. Two thousand vials of rabies immunoglobulin have arrived in Ukraine, which will be distributed among the regions in the near future to ensure medical facilities that provide care to patients at risk of rabies infection. The Ministry of Health reported that the delivery was made possible thanks to the support of the Georgian government and the World Health Organization. This delivery was made possible thanks to the support of the Georgian government and the World Health Organization. The received rabies immunoglobulin will be promptly distributed among the regions so that medical facilities providing anti-rabies care have the necessary resources to treat patients according to medical indications. – Ihor Kuzin Context and actions for rabies prevention In the health system at the level of regional medical facilities, designated rabies care centers have been established: they are stocked with vaccines, the doctors at the centers provide medical care to patients who seek help after bites, scratches, or exposure to animals; they perform initial treatment; prescribe and administer courses of anti-rabies vaccinations as needed. Rabies is a particularly dangerous infectious disease that can be completely prevented with timely medical help and the implementation of post-exposure prophylaxis. The basis of rabies prevention is the rabies vaccine, which is used in the vast majority of cases after bites or contacts with potentially infected animals. Rabies immunoglobulin is not required for everyone after a bite or exposure to animals – only in cases of high risk of infection. In particular, with severe or multiple bites, bites to the head, neck, hands, or exposure of mucous membranes. The choice of prophylaxis scheme is determined solely by the physician based on an assessment of risk for each specific case: to prescribe only a course of rabies vaccination or, if necessary, to combine vaccination with administration of rabies immunoglobulin. According to the Ministry of Health, in May the regions received over 42,000 doses of the rabies vaccine. Cases of rabies infections are rising, in particular due to military actions in the country. This delivery underscores the importance of coordinating efforts in the health sector and supporting international partners in the fight against the virus. We recommend paying attention to: Over 260 people in Donetsk sought medical care for animal bites in Q1 2026; health authorities say vaccines and immunoglobulin were provided and urge continued vigilance against rabies. The Ministry of Health delivered 42,650 rabies vaccine doses to regional Centers for Disease Control; doses will be distributed to anti-rabies clinics to strengthen post-exposure prophylaxis. Ukraine’s health committee urges modernizing tobacco legislation to cover snus, e-cigarettes, and other nicotine products, aiming to curb youth targeting and strengthen public health protections. Australian land forces join Norway led Legio training in Poland to boost Ukrainian readiness Saturday, 18 July 2026, 06:45 Australian instructors embedded in the Kudu operation have joined Norway’s Legio training in Poland. The collaboration pushes tactics to the limit to expose weaknesses before combat. As reported by the online media Ukrinform. Representatives of the Australian Army’s Land Forces joined the training of Ukrainian defenders in Poland. According to official information from the General Staff of the Armed Forces of Ukraine’s press service, this was confirmed on their page. The personnel of the Kudu training operation of the Australian Army have joined the Legio training operation under the leadership of the Norwegian Armed Forces Forsvaret in Poland to jointly conduct high-quality training of the Ukrainian Armed Forces, aimed at increasing the effectiveness of Ukrainian units on the battlefield – General Staff of the Armed Forces of Ukraine Within the training, Ukrainian and Australian instructors work side by side, creating highly realistic scenarios. A hallmark of this course is testing tactical plans to the brink of mistakes in a safe training environment. Such an approach allows early identification of weaknesses, a detailed analysis of unit actions, and rapid elimination of deficiencies even before entering real combat zones. In addition to practicing practical skills, the collaboration among allies aims to foster an unbreakable combat spirit and psychological resilience. The training helps Ukrainian defenders rapidly adapt to the changing conditions of modern confrontation, develop flexible tactical thinking, and step by step form a hardened warrior mindset today to confidently defeat the enemy tomorrow. Under the motto “adapt and win,” Australian soldiers together with multinational partners are doing everything possible to help Ukraine win the war on its own terms. As explained by the General Staff, military assistance from partners is implemented through several key initiatives. Kudu and Legio Initiative: Figures and Objectives Operation Kudu is a large-scale program of the Australian Defence Forces, within which Australian instructors train Ukrainian servicemen. Operation Legio, led by the Norwegian Armed Forces on Polish territory, focuses on providing in-depth and specialized training. The integration of the Australian Kudu contingent into the Norwegian Legio operation is an example of effective alliance coordination for high-quality training of Ukrainian forces in Europe, the General Staff summed up. The Legio initiative, launched in 2024 under Norway’s leadership, brought together nine Nordic and Baltic countries: Denmark, Estonia, Finland, Iceland, Latvia, Lithuania, Norway, Poland, and Sweden. The initiative provides: training of Ukrainian military personnel by partner-country instructors; equipping various Ukrainian units with weapons in amounts equivalent to brigade sizes, in accordance with NATO standards; training on Polish territory; strengthening partnership ties between Ukraine and the Nordic and Baltic countries in the field of security and defense. In summary, the General Staff stresses that the united efforts of the allies enable high-quality training of Ukrainian troops in Europe and strengthen defense cooperation with regional countries. 正文要点中文精炼科罗拉多州的一名兽医组织了一场虚拟 5K 活动,为华盛顿州立大学在坦桑尼亚的狂犬病控制项目筹集近 14,000 美元。受坦桑尼亚之旅的启发,首届活动旨在支持犬类狂犬病疫苗接种,并旨在成为一项年度传统。这些资金将有助于华盛顿州立大学在该地区持续进行的狂犬病控制工作。没有与本次筹款活动相关的病例、死亡或接触报告。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026-07-16。事件日期:所提供证据中未报告。
地点与对象
美国(科罗拉多州)和坦桑尼亚。受影响人群:坦桑尼亚的狂犬病控制目标。
事件
科罗拉多州的一名兽医组织了一场虚拟 5K 活动,为华盛顿州立大学 (WSU) 在坦桑尼亚的狂犬病控制项目筹集资金。据报道,该活动是受坦桑尼亚之旅启发而发起的一项首创活动,并计划使其成为一年一度的传统活动。这是报告的筹款活动,而不是疫情或已确认的公共卫生事件。
规模、影响与风险
筹集资金:近14,000美元。没有病例、死亡或暴露的报告。该活动旨在支持坦桑尼亚的犬类狂犬病疫苗接种。没有明确规定风险级别。
应对、状态与不确定性
筹集的资金将用于 WSU 在坦桑尼亚的狂犬病控制项目。提供的证据中没有报告进一步的调查、测试或官方回应细节。该活动被描述为首届活动,并计划每年重复举办。

渥太华县发现狂犬病阳性蝙蝠

Rabies-positive bat detected in Ottawa County
正文要点中文精炼当地官员证实,密歇根州渥太华县报告了 2026 年第一例狂犬病阳性蝙蝠。虽然没有人类病例或接触记录,但当局敦促居民在室内遇到蝙蝠时要小心谨慎。该公告遵循针对蝙蝠遭遇的标准公共卫生指南,强调狂犬病传播的风险。除一般建议外,该县没有提供有关测试、调查范围或预防措施的更多详细信息。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026-07-15。事件日期:所提供证据中未报告。
地点与对象
美国密歇根州渥太华县。受影响人群:密歇根人(公众)。
事件
在渥太华县发现了一只狂犬病呈阳性的蝙蝠。该事件已由当地官员报告并得到证实。该报告鼓励公众采取预防措施。
规模、影响与风险
今年该县首次发现狂犬病阳性动物。所提供的证据中没有报告人类病例或暴露情况。
应对、状态与不确定性
如果在家中发现蝙蝠,官员们鼓励采取预防措施。提供的证据中没有报告进一步的调查细节、测试方案或接触者追踪。来源限制:联合摘要。

B 常规重要

约贝市爆发狂犬病:达马鲁 (Damaturu) 遭狗严重袭击,5 人入院 - Streamlinefeed.co.ke

Rabies Outbreak in Yobe: 5 Hospitalized Following Severe Dog Attacks in Damaturu - streamlinefeed.co.ke
正文要点中文精炼尼日利亚约贝州达马图鲁的马拉姆马塔里社区发生严重狗袭击事件,五名居民正在住院接受紧急狂犬病治疗。在实验室确认攻击狗患有狂犬病后,约贝州政府启动了兽医快速反应小组。受害者遭受了深度撕裂伤,正在接受暴露后预防(PEP)以预防这种普遍致命的疾病。公共卫生官员强调立即清洗伤口、紧急医疗护理和社区警惕。这次疫情凸显了半城市社区由于未接种疫苗的流浪犬和公众意识低下而长期存在的脆弱性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表日期:2026-07-13。事件发生于尼日利亚约贝州达马图鲁,但所提供的证据中未报告具体事件日期。
地点与对象
尼日利亚约贝州马拉姆马塔里社区达马图鲁。受影响人群:五名居民因狗袭击而住院。
事件
五名居民在遭受严重的狗袭击后被送往医院接受紧急狂犬病治疗。约贝州政府启动了兽医快速反应小组。据报道,该事件正在爆发,正在调查中,实验室已确认攻击狗患有狂犬病。
规模、影响与风险
5 例住院病例接受暴露后预防 (PEP)。由于一旦出现临床狂犬病症状,死亡率为 100%,因此该风险被描述为高风险。这次疫情凸显了尼日利亚城郊社区对人畜共患病原体的脆弱性。
应对、状态与不确定性
约贝州政府和兽医团队正在开展遏制、宣传活动并分发 PEP 疫苗。公共卫生指令包括立即清洗伤口、紧急医疗干预、动物观察和社区报告。未解决:应急响应之外的长期预防策略。来源限制:联合摘要不适用。证据来自信誉良好的媒体。
